2020 PERFORMANCE REVIEW                                                 2020年度業績回顧

In 2020, the outbreak of COVID-19 pandemic has spread throughout        2020年，新冠肺炎疫情肆虐全球，世界
the world and severely disrupted the global economy, which caused the   經濟陷入嚴重衰退，外部環境更加複雜
external environment to be more sophisticated and severe. In general,   嚴峻。但總的來看，2020年中國統籌疫
China has attained remarkable results in coordinating the pandemic      情防控和保障民生發展工作成效顯著，
control and protecting public wellbeing in 2020. The operation of       經濟運行穩定恢復，就業民生保障有
economy rebounded steadily, while employment and public wellbeing       力，社會發展主要目標任務完成情況好
were guaranteed and the completion of the major goals of social         於預期。2020年初，餐飲服務市場受疫
development achieved better than expected. In early 2020, catering      情影響巨大，面臨嚴重經營壓力。但隨
service market was affected significantly by the pandemic, which        著疫情「常態化」防控的有效進行，餐飲
caused a severe burden in operation. Nevertheless, along with the       行業亦於年內逐步恢復。相比之下，複
effective performance of “ normalized ” pandemic protection, the        合調味料及方便速食行業卻受益於疫情
catering industry has also gradually recovered. By contrast, compound   防控下催生的「宅經濟」，於2020年上半
condiment and convenient ready-to-eat food industry have both           年呈現出高速增長態勢，但於下半年需
experienced accelerating demand growth in the first half of 2020,       求逐步回落。
benefiting from the “Stay-at-Home Economy” brought by the pandemic
control, however, those demand gradually declined toward the second
half.

     The Group is committed to achieving high standards of corporate           本集團致力達致高水準的企業管治，務
     governance to safeguard the interests of the Shareholders and to          求保障股東權益及提高企業價值、問責
     enhance corporate value, accountability and transparency.                 性及透明度。

     Governance Code and also the corporate governance report as               業管治守則的原則及企業管治報告。
     contained in Appendix 14 to the Listing Rules.

     corporate governance and two levels of corporate governance               及兩個層面的企業管治常規如下：
     practices, as follows:

     (a)   code provisions, which listed issuers are expected to comply with   (a)   守則條文，期望上市發行人遵守或
           or to give considered reasons for deviation; and                          就偏離行為提供審慎考慮的理由；
                                                                                     及

     (b)   recommended best practices for guidance only, which listed          (b)   建議最佳常規，僅屬指引，鼓勵上
           issuers are encouraged to comply with.                                    市發行人遵守。

     The Company ’s corporate governance practices are based on                本公司的企業管治常規乃以企業管治守
     the principles and the code provisions as set out in the Corporate        則所載原則及守則條文為依據。
     Governance Code.

     Corporate Governance Code and the Company was in compliance with          且本公司於截至2020年12月31日止年度

     has entered into a service contract with the Company                      與本公司訂立服務合約，據

     case may be), subject to re-election as and when required                 定），可根據組織章程細則於

     accordance with the terms and conditions of the service                   約的條款及條件終止或由其中

           separate and should not be performed by the same individual.                 分，並不應由同一人兼任。

           Mr. Shi Yonghong had assumed both the roles of the chairman                  由於施永宏先生在海底撈集團的食
           and the chief executive officer of the Company for the period                品和餐飲服務行業以及管理經驗方

           experience and knowledge in the food and the catering service                高本公司營運效率，故彼於2018年
           industry and management experience within the Haidilao Group                 12月9日至2020年3月25日期間兼
           and in order to improve the efficiency of the operations of the              任本公司主席及首席執行官。
           Company.

           In view of the ever-changing business environment in which the               鑑於本集團經營的業務環境不斷變
           Group operates, the chairman and the chief executive officer must            化，主席及首席執行官必須精通中
           be proficient in the Chinese condiment market and be sensitive               國調味品市場，並對市場變化敏
           to market changes in order to promote the businesses of the                  感，以促進本集團的業務。因此，
           Group. The Board thus considered a segregation of the role of the            董事會認為，主席及首席執行官的
           chairman and chief executive officer might create unnecessary                角色分離可能會為本集團的日常營
           costs for the daily operations of the Group.                                 運帶來不必要的成本。

     K.    SHAREHOLDERS’ RIGHTS                                                   K.   股東權利

           To safeguard the Shareholders’ interests and rights, separate               為保障股東權益及權利，各重大事
           resolutions are proposed at general meetings on each substantial            宜（包括重選個別董事）以獨立決議
           issue, including the re-election of individual Directors. Meanwhile,        案形式於股東大會上提呈，而且股
           procedures are in place for the Shareholders to (i) convene an              東可根據有關程序提出(i)召開股東
           extraordinary general meeting; (ii) direct their enquiries to the           特別大會；(ii)向董事會查詢；及(iii)

     Risk of Product Quality                                                   產品質量風險

     The quality and safety of our products are very important to              產品的質量及安全對我們持續經營

     sufficiently implemented, quality or food safety issues may arise.        系出現控制不到位，則有可能出現
     With the increase of the number of our self-owned factories and           質量或食品安全問題，且隨著自有
     OEM factories, if the management and control is poor, there will          工廠及OEM工廠數量的增加，管控
     be food safety risks and product quality risks, which will have a         不善會存在食品安全風險和產品質
     material adverse impact on the Company’s brand, reputation and            量風險，進而對本公司的品牌、聲

Risk of Environmental Protection in Production                          環保生產風險

Some individual factories of the Group are in close proximity           本集團個別工廠位於居民區附近，
to the residential area, and the waste gas treatment facilities of      而工廠的廢氣處理設施對於異味的
the factories have a relatively low treatment efficiency in odour,      處理效率較低，正常生產過程中產
and considerable odour may arise from the normal production             生異味較大，對周邊企業及居民造
process and such odour may cause nuisance to the surrounding            成異味困擾，可能會造成環保投訴
enterprises and residents that may result in complaint cases of         案件，進而對本公司的聲譽造成負
environmental protection and, in turn, may have adverse effect on       面影響。
the reputation of the Company.

Response Measures                                                       應對措施

indicators for control of waste gas and odour, all factories are        指標，並要求各工廠加強日常維保
required to enhance daily maintenance and regular overhaul              及定期全面檢測等工作，保證廢氣
inspection to ensure that the emissions of waste gas have complied      排放符合環保要求。同時，制度要
with the environmental protection requirements. Meanwhile,              求新工廠在設計階段便需考慮採取
the system also requires that any new factory must consider to          有效的廢氣處理工藝和設備，並逐
adopt effective waste gas treatment technique and equipment at          步更新舊工廠的廢氣處理設備，確
the design stage, and the waste gas treatment equipment of old          保最終排放的廢氣滿足並逐步高於
factories must be upgraded gradually to ensure that the ultimate        排放標準。目前而言，本集團的新
emissions of waste gas will meet and may be even better than the        工廠主要選址在各地的工業園區，
required emission standards. At present, new factories of the Group     盡量從源頭杜絕異味投訴等環保問
are mainly built on sites inside industrial parks of various regions,   題的產生。

      環境、社會及管治報告



      ABOUT THE REPORT                                                            關於本報告

      YIHAI INTERNATIONAL HOLDING LTD. (hereinafter “ Yihai ”, “ the              頤海國際控股有限公司（下稱「頤海」  「本
      Group”, or “we”) is pleased to present our report on Environmental,         集團」或「我們」）欣然呈列2020年度之環
      Social and Governance (ESG) for the year of 2020 (“the Report”). This       境、社會及管治報告（下稱「本報告」）。
      is the fifth consecutive ESG report we have disclosed to the public         本報告系我們連續第五年向社會發佈本
      regarding our ESG management philosophy and performance of our              集團在環境、社會及管治（下稱「ESG」）
      practices. We aim to disclose in this report the concepts and practices     方面的管理理念與績效表現。我們希望
      on sustainable development of the Group in a transparent manner and         本報告能傳遞本集團的可持續發展理念
      enhance the mutual understanding and communication of the various           與管理實踐，促進各利益相關方對本集
      stakeholders of the Group.                                                  團的了解和溝通。


      PREPARATION BASIS OF THE REPORT                                             報告編製標準

      The Report is prepared in accordance with Appendix 27 Environmental,        本報告編製依據為香港交易及結算所有
      Social and Governance Reporting Guide (the “ESG Reporting Guide”)           限公司發佈的《香港聯合交易所有限公司

In 2020, the Group introduced the concept of “ industry centre ”.           2020 年，本集團提出「產業中心」的概
Guiding by this concept, we communicated and cooperated with local          念，通過與當地政府的溝通協作，給予
government in a move to provide financial and technical supports            供應商資金和技術支持，鼓勵各供應商
to suppliers while encouraging suppliers to set up production lines         就近建設生產線，使生產本地化，既能

      Improvement of Waste Water Treatment                                       改進污水處理

      Due to the business nature, the Group’s sewage arises from the wok         基於業務特點，本集團污水主要來源於

      and discharge vacuum system and oil-water separation system to             空集中吸排系統和油水分離系統，以避
      avoid direct discharge of waste water containing waste oil. In 2020,       免含廢油的污水直接排放。2020 年，
      Zhengzhou Factory was equipped with grease traps, while Ma’anshan          鄭州工廠增設隔油池，馬鞍山工廠增設
      Factory was furnished with grease traps and oil-water separation           隔油池和油水分離裝置，在降低污水處
      devices. These equipment or devices can reduce the burden of sewage        理站負擔的同時提升了污水處理效果。

RISK-ORIENTED DEVOTION OF SUPPLY CHAIN SYSTEM                                     風險為尺，深耕供應鏈體系

An important component of the development strategy of the Group is                「注重食品安全質量，防範原材料供應風
to “emphasise safe and quality food and prevent risks in the supply               險」是本集團發展戰略中的重要組成部
of raw materials”. The Group has established a series of supply chain             分。本集團建立了一系列供應鏈管理制
management systems to develop a responsible supply chain and to                   度，力求打造責任供應鏈，推動整個供
promote the improvement and development of the entire supply chain                應鏈體系的進步與發展。
system.

Strict Selection Criteria for Suppliers                                           嚴把供應商准入關口

The Group chooses suppliers in strict compliance with requirements                本集團嚴格遵守《中華人民共和國反不正
of laws and regulations such as the Anti-unfair Competition Law                   當競爭法》等法律法規的要求，並結合制
of the People ’s Republic of China , combined with the formulated                 定的《供應商准入審核管理規定》對供貨

      Refer to Notes 2.13, 4.1 and 11 to the consolidated         請參閱綜合財務報表附註2.13、4.1及11。
      financial statements.

      Inventories are carried at the lower of cost and            存貨乃按其成本與可變現淨值（即為預估的銷售價格減去
      net realisable value, being estimated selling price         預估的銷售成本）孰低者列賬，適用時，確認減值準備以
      less estimated selling costs, where applicable, and         將存貨撇減至其可變現淨值。
      impairment provision is recognised to write down the
      inventories to their net realisable value.

      As at 31 December 2020, the carrying amount of              於 2020 年 12 月 31 日， 貴集團存貨的賬面值為人民幣
      the Group ’s inventories was RMB406,033,000 and             406,033,000元及並無確認存貨減值準備。
      no provision for impairment of inventories has been
      recognised.

      The estimation of impairment provision for inventories      存貨的減值準備估計涉及管理層重大判斷及估計，該等判
      involves significant management ’s judgment and             斷及估計需考慮庫齡結構、過往經驗、預估未來售價以及
      estimates based on the consideration of certain             銷售開支等若干關鍵因素。
      key factors such as aging profile, prior experiences,
      estimated future selling prices and selling expenses.

      The impairment assessment of inventories is an area of      鑑於存貨可變現淨值的估計受限於估計高度不確定性以及
      focus for us given the estimation of net realisable value   存貨結額金額龐大，存貨減值評估是我們關注的範疇。
      of inventories is subject to high degree of estimation
      uncertainty and the significant amount of the inventory
      balance.

How our audit addressed the Key Audit Matter
我們的審計如何處理關鍵審計事項

Impairment provision for inventories
存貨減值準備

Our audit procedures relating to impairment provision       我們針對存貨減值準備的審計程式包括以下各項：
for inventories included the following:

•   We obtained and understood the Group’s inventory        •   我們獲得並了解了貴集團的存貨準備政策。
    provision policy.

•   We understood and evaluated the relevant                •   我們對管理層就存貨可變現淨值採取的相關內部監控
    management’s internal controls and assessment               及評估程序進行了解及評估，並通過考慮估計的不確
    process of net realisable value of inventories and          定程度及其他固有風險因素（例如複雜性、主觀性、
    assessed the inherent risk of material misstatement         變動及受管理層偏見或欺詐影響的程度）水平，評估
    by considering the degree of estimation uncertainty         重大錯誤陳述的固有風險。
    and level of other inherent risk factors such as
    complexity, subjectivity, changes and susceptibility
    to management bias or fraud.

•   We evaluated, on sample basis, the assumptions in       •   我們參考歷史記錄及年結日後的銷售交易，在抽樣的
    estimating future selling prices and selling expenses       基礎上對預估未來售價及銷售開支的假設進行了評
    by reference to historical records and sales                估。
    transactions after year end.

•   On sample basis, we tested the aging profile of the     •   在抽樣的基礎上，我們對年結日的原材料及產成品的
    year end raw materials and finished goods.                  庫齡結構進行了測試。

•   We observed physical condition of inventories           •   我們在存貨盤點期間查看了存貨的物理狀況，以確定
    during stocktake to identify if any inventories were        存貨是否出現滯銷、損壞或報廢。
    slow-moving, damaged, or obsolete.

Based on the above, we considered that management’s         基於上述各項，我們認為管理層在評估存貨減值準備時所
judgement and estimates applied in the assessment of        作出的判斷及估計有可得的證據及所進行的程序支持。
impairment provision for inventories were supportable
by the evidence obtained and procedures performed.

          (i)   Subsidiaries                                                       (i)   附屬公司

                has rights to, variable returns from its involvement with                透過參與實體業務而享

                through its power to direct the activities of the entity.                可變回報且有能力透過
                Subsidiaries are fully consolidated from the date on                     其指揮該實體業務的權
                which control is transferred to the Group. They are                      力影響該等回報時，則
                deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                        本集團控制該實體。附

                                                                                         本集團之日起全部合併
                                                                                         入賬，並自控制終止之
                                                                                         日起取消合併。

                to Note 2.4).                                                            附註2.4）。

                Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised                      集團公司間的公司間交
                gains on transactions between group companies are                        易、結餘及未變現交易

                  (ii)   Changes in ownership interests                                      (ii)   所有權權益變動

                         The Group treats transactions with non-controlling                         本集團將不導致喪失控
                         interests that do not result in a loss of control as                       制權的非控股權益交易
                         transactions with equity owners of the Group. A                            視作與本集團權益擁有
                         change in ownership interest results in an adjustment                      人的交易。所有權權益
                         between the carrying amounts of the controlling                            變動導致控股與非控股
                         and non-controlling interests to reflect their relative                    權益賬面值的調整，以
                         interests in the subsidiary. Any difference between the                    反映其於附屬公司的相
                         amount of the adjustment to non-controlling interests                      關權益。非控股權益調
                         and any consideration paid or received is recognised in                    整數額與任何已付或已
                         a separate reserve within equity attributable to owners                    收代價之間的任何差額
                         of the Company.                                                            於本公司擁有人應佔權
                                                                                                    益中的獨立儲備內確認。


                         When the Group ceases to consolidate an investment                         當本集團因喪失控制
                         because of a loss of control, joint control or significant                 權、共同控制權或重大
                         influence, any retained interest in the entity is                          影響力而停止綜合入賬
                         remeasured to its fair value with the change in carrying                   一項投資時，於實體的
                         amount recognised in profit or loss. This fair value                       任何保留權益重新按公
                         becomes the initial carrying amount for the purposes                       平值計量，而賬面值變
                         of subsequently accounting for the retained interest                       動於損益確認。就其後
                         as an associate, joint venture or financial asset. In                      入賬列作聯營公司、合
                         addition, any amounts previously recognised in other                       營企業或金融資產的保
                         comprehensive income in respect of that entity are                         留權益而言，該公平值
                         accounted for as if the Group had directly disposed                        為初始賬面值。此外，
                         of the related assets or liabilities. This may mean that                   先前於其他全面收入就
                         amounts previously recognised in other comprehensive                       該實體確認的任何金額
                         income are reclassified to profit or loss or transferred                   按猶如本集團已直接出
                         to another category of equity as specified/permitted by                    售有關資產或負債的方
                         applicable IFRSs.                                                          式入賬。這意味著先前
                                                                                                    於其他全面收入確認的
                                                                                                    金額重新分類至損益或

     2.10 Impairment of non-financial assets                                      2.10 非金融資產減值

          Intangible assets that have an indefinite useful life are                  可使用年期不限定的無形資產
          not subject to amortisation and are tested annually for                    毋須攤銷，但每年須就減值進
          impairment, or more frequently if events or changes in                     行測試，或於發生事項或情況
          circumstances indicate that they might be impaired. Other                  出現變化而顯示可能出現減值
          assets are tested for impairment whenever events or                        時作出更頻密的減值測試。其
          changes in circumstances indicate that the carrying amount                 他資產會於發生事項或情況出
          may not be recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised                   現變化而顯示其賬面值未必能
          for the amount by which the asset ’s carrying amount                       夠收回時，進行減值測試。減
          exceeds its recoverable amount. The recoverable amount                     值虧損按有關資產賬面值超逾
          is the higher of an asset’s fair value less costs of disposal              其可收回金額的數額確認。可
          and value in use. For the purposes of assessing impairment,                收回金額為資產的公平值減出
          assets are grouped at the lowest levels for which there                    售成本與使用價值兩者的較高
          are separately identifiable cash inflows which are largely                 者。就減值評估而言，資產按
          independent of the cash inflows from other assets or groups                獨立可識別現金流入的最低
          of assets (cash-generating units). Non-financial assets other              分類組合（現金產生單位）分
          than goodwill that suffered an impairment are reviewed                     類，其所產生的現金流入基本
          for possible reversal of the impairment at the end of each                 上獨立於其他資產或資產組
          reporting period.                                                          別。出現減值的非金融資產
                                                                                    （商譽除外）於各報告期末檢討
                                                                                     是否可能撥回減值。

          Deferred income tax                                                         遞延所得稅

     2.23 Leases                                                                2.23 租賃

          Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a                      在本集團預期可使用租賃資產
          corresponding liability at the date at which the leased asset            之日，租賃應確認為使用權資
          is available for use by the Group.                                       產和相應負債。

          Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease                           合約可包含租賃及非租賃部
          components. The Group allocates the consideration in the                 分。本集團根據其相對獨立的
          contract to the lease and non-lease components based on                  價格將合約的代價分配至租賃
          their relative stand-alone prices. However, for leases of real           及非租賃部分。然而，就本集
          estate for which the Group is a lessee, it has elected not               團作為承租人租賃房地產而
          to separate lease and non-lease components and instead                   言，其已選擇不區分租賃及非
          accounts for these as a single lease component.                          租賃部分，而是將該等租賃入
                                                                                   賬作為單一租賃部分。

          Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and                    租賃條款按個別基準協商，並
          contain a wide range of different terms and conditions. The              包含多種不同的條款和條件。
          lease agreements do not impose any covenants other than                  除出租人持有租賃資產的抵押
          the security interests in the leased assets that are held by             權益外，租賃協議不施加任何
          the lessor. Leased assets may not be used as security for                契諾。租賃資產不得用作借款
          borrowing purposes.                                                      抵押。

          Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially                租賃產生的資產和負債在現值

                                                                                   值：

          •    variable lease payment that are based on an index or a              •      取決於指數或比率的可
               rate;                                                                      變租賃付款額；

     2.23 Leases (Continued)                                                      2.23 租賃（續）

          Lease payments are allocated between the liability and                     租賃付款額均在相應負債與融
          finance cost. The finance cost is charged to profit or loss                資成本之間分攤。融資成本在
          over the lease period so as to produce a constant periodic                 租賃期限內計入損益，以使各
          rate of interest on the remaining balance of the liability for             期負債餘額產生的利息率保持
          each period.                                                               一致。

          Right-of-use assets are measured at cost comprising the                    使用權資產按照成本計量，包
          following:                                                                 括：

          •    the amount of the initial measurement of lease liability;             •   租賃負債初始計量金
                                                                                         額；

          •    any lease payments made at or before the commencement                 •   在租賃期開始日或之前
               date less any lease incentives received;                                  支付的租賃付款額，扣
                                                                                         除收到的租賃激勵；

          •    any initial direct costs; and                                         •   任何初始直接費用；及

          •    restoration costs.                                                    •   復原成本。

          Right-of-use asset are generally depreciated over the shorter              使用權資產一般於資產的可使
          of the asset’s useful life and the lease term on a straight-line           用年期及租賃期（以較短者為
          basis. If the Group is reasonably certain to exercise a                    準）內按直線法予以折舊。倘
          purchase option, the right-of-use asset is depreciated over                本集團合理確定行使購買選擇
          the underlying asset’s useful life.                                        權，則使用權資產於相關資產
                                                                                     的可使用年期內予以折舊。

          Payments associated with short-term leases and all leases                  與短期租賃以及所有低價值資
          of low-value assets are recognised on a straight-line basis                產租賃相關的付款按直線法於
          as an expense in profit or loss. Short-term leases are leases              損益確認為開支。短期租賃指
          with a lease term of 12 months or less. Low-value assets                   租賃期為 12 個月或以下的租

                  assets.                                                                   認。

4.   CRITICAL ESTIMATES AND JUDGEMENTS                                  4.   重大估計及判斷

     The preparation of financial statements requires the use of             編製財務報表需要使用會計估計，
     accounting estimates which, by definition, will seldom equal the        顧名思義，該等估計甚少符合實際
     actual results. Management also needs to exercise judgement in          結果。管理層亦須在應用本集團會
     applying the Group’s accounting policies.                               計政策時作出判斷。

     Estimates and judgements are continually evaluated. They are            估計及判斷會予以持續評估，且基
     based on historical experience and other factors, including             於歷史經驗及其他因素，包括對未
     expectations of future events that may have a financial impact          來事件的預期，而該等事件可能對
     on the entity and that are believed to be reasonable under the          實體造成財務影響且據信在某些情
     circumstances.                                                          況下屬合理。

     4.1 Critical accounting estimates                                       4.1 重大會計估計

         (a)   Net realisable value of inventories                             (a)   存貨的可變現淨值

               Net realisable value of inventories is the estimated                  存貨的可變現淨值按日
               selling price in the ordinary course of business, less                常業務過程中的估計售
               estimated costs of completion and selling expenses.                   價減估計完工成本及銷
               These estimates are based on the current market                       售開支計算。該等估計
               condition and historical experience of manufacturing                  乃基於當前市況以及製
               and selling products of similar nature. Management                    造及銷售類似性質產品
               reassesses these estimates at each balance sheet                      的過往經驗作出。管理
               date.                                                                 層於各資產負債表日期
                                                                                     重估該等估計。




                                                                                     2020 Annual Report 年 度 報 告   223
      Notes to the Consolidated Financial Statements
      綜合財務報表附註

            4.1 Critical accounting estimates (Continued)                                   4.1 重大會計估計（續）

33. RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS                                             33. 關聯方交易

   Parties are considered to be related if one party has the ability,           如一方有能力直接或間接控制另一
   directly or indirectly, to control the other party or exercise               方或對另一方作出財務及經營決策
   significant influence over the other party in making financial               行使重大影響力，則各方被視為有
   and operation decisions. Parties are also considered to be                   關聯。如彼等受共同控制，各方亦
   related if they are subject to common control. Members of key                被視為有關聯。本集團主要管理層
   management and their close family member of the Group are also               成員及其近親亦被視為關聯方。
   considered as related parties.

   Save as disclosed elsewhere in these consolidated financial                  除該等綜合財務報表其他部分所披
   statements, the Group has the following related party transactions,          露者外，本集團有以下關聯方交
   and they were carried out in the ordinary course of the Group’s              易，該等交易於本集團日常業務過
   business and were determined based on mutually agreed terms.                 程中進行且基於相互協定的條款釐
                                                                                定。
